# All-hypothesis evidence ledger

These are identifiable research records, not independent statistical trials. Batches are expanded; formal reruns and portfolio combinations remain labeled. Recorded metrics are copied, not recomputed. Fixed-notional P&L is not compounded cash and dollar amounts across different sizing models must not be pooled.

Classification: economic = tested economic/prediction failure; robustness = positive portions but benchmark/generalization/cost/concentration failure; measurement = unstable or invalid observable; data = necessary observation missing; semantic = duplicate or unidentified causal sign; protocol = cadence/component/mandate gate; pending = no completed tradable-edge test; scope = outside day trading; diagnostic = input/benchmark audit; outside_daytrade_positive = positive nonintraday evidence.

### SPY-SH:counterauction_effort_failure — Counterauction Effort Failure

**economic; Historical P&L.** development: fixed-notional P&L $-11.05; ending cash $89.45; PF 0.530. The recorded marketable-execution rule failed its frozen economic and/or robustness gates. The name does not establish the proposed institutional flow.

**What could change the evidence:** No identified missing field explains this rejection. Preserve the frozen result; new signal evidence requires a distinct measurement and fresh validation.

**Original claim (not established causality):** If the second half of the opening auction spends more volume per point pushing against the first-half move yet cannot reclaim the cash open, the counterauction is being absorbed and the original inventory should resume.

**Relationship:** Related variants share data and multiple-testing exposure; not independent trials.

**Sources:** [spy_sh_fractional_hypotheses_results_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/spy_sh_fractional_hypotheses_results_v1.json), [spy_sh_fractional_hypotheses_prereg_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/spy_sh_fractional_hypotheses_prereg_v1.json)

Recorded failed gates: gate_passed: False; gate_failures: ['frequency', 'aggregate_pnl', 'profit_factor', 'each_year', 'both_directions'].

### SPY-SH:opening_volume_scar_parallax — Opening Volume Scar Parallax

**economic; Historical P&L.** development: fixed-notional P&L $-26.61; ending cash $76.38; PF 0.533. The recorded marketable-execution rule failed its frozen economic and/or robustness gates. The name does not establish the proposed institutional flow.

**What could change the evidence:** No identified missing field explains this rejection. Preserve the frozen result; new signal evidence requires a distinct measurement and fresh validation.

**Original claim (not established causality):** When the opening close and total volume-weighted center migrate together but the single highest-volume minute remains priced across the cash open, the heaviest local inventory is trapped behind accepted value.

**economic; Historical P&L.** development: fixed-notional P&L $-1.85; ending cash $98.16; PF 0.000. The recorded marketable-execution rule failed its frozen economic and/or robustness gates. The name does not establish the proposed institutional flow.

**What could change the evidence:** No identified missing field explains this rejection. Preserve the frozen result; new signal evidence requires a distinct measurement and fresh validation.

**Original claim (not established causality):** If the opening auction explores farther in one direction but both accepted value and the terminal print finish across the open in the opposite direction, ownership has transferred away from the larger failed excursion.

### V1:daily_reset_recoil — Daily Reset Recoil

**economic; Historical P&L.** development: fixed-notional P&L $-7.25; ending cash $90.82; PF 0.950. The recorded marketable-execution rule failed its frozen economic and/or robustness gates. The name does not establish the proposed institutional flow.

**What could change the evidence:** No identified missing field explains this rejection. Preserve the frozen result; new signal evidence requires a distinct measurement and fresh validation.

**Original claim (not established causality):** Leveraged ETF exposure is reset around the prior close. When the cash open gaps away from that reset anchor and the first half-hour counterauction cannot return through it, inherited positioning should reassert in the gap direction.

**Relationship:** Related variants share data and multiple-testing exposure; not independent trials.

**Sources:** [upro_spxu_creative_hypotheses_results_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/upro_spxu_creative_hypotheses_results_v1.json), [upro_spxu_creative_hypotheses_prereg_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/upro_spxu_creative_hypotheses_prereg_v1.json)

### V1:reset_corridor_escape — Reset Corridor Escape

**economic; Historical P&L.** development: fixed-notional P&L $-16.16; ending cash $83.87; PF 0.842. The recorded marketable-execution rule failed its frozen economic and/or robustness gates. The name does not establish the proposed institutional flow.

**What could change the evidence:** No identified missing field explains this rejection. Preserve the frozen result; new signal evidence requires a distinct measurement and fresh validation.

**Original claim (not established causality):** The prior close is the daily leverage-reset anchor and the current opening center is the new cash-auction anchor. If the opening traverses both anchors and then closes outside their corridor, one participant group has carried price through both definitions of value.

**Relationship:** Related variants share data and multiple-testing exposure; not independent trials.

**Sources:** [upro_spxu_creative_hypotheses_results_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/upro_spxu_creative_hypotheses_results_v1.json), [upro_spxu_creative_hypotheses_prereg_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/upro_spxu_creative_hypotheses_prereg_v1.json)

### V1:closing_rebalance_torque — Closing Rebalance Torque

**economic; Historical P&L.** development: fixed-notional P&L $-7.47; ending cash $91.08; PF 0.950. The recorded marketable-execution rule failed its frozen economic and/or robustness gates. The name does not establish the proposed institutional flow.

**What could change the evidence:** No identified missing field explains this rejection. Preserve the frozen result; new signal evidence requires a distinct measurement and fresh validation.

**Original claim (not established causality):** Three-times long and inverse funds must adjust derivative exposure as the index moves. When both the full day and the 13:30-14:29 participant window point the same way, closing rebalance demand may add torque in that direction.

### V15 — Listing-Venue Participation Tilt

**measurement; Pre-return screen.** Arca volume was not contained in the comparison feed; median supposed shares exceeded 100%. 612/666 signals selected UPRO. The denominator did not measure consolidated participation.

### V19 — Premarket Wrapper Premium

**economic; Development.** Ending cash $62.36, PF 0.887; annual, sleeve, cost and benchmark gates failed.

### V32 — BTC Attention Gate times SPY Gap

**economic; Development.** Ending cash $64.50; lagged placebo and unfiltered gap controls were stronger; independent overlap lost.

**What could change the evidence:** Contemporaneous BTC spot activity did not identify usable SPY information.

### V41 — Closing Market-Urgency Hedge Window

**protocol; Pre-return screen.** 86 development features, 11 executable overlap signals; cadence, side balance and execution coverage failed. No P&L.

### V62 — Creation-Conditioned Wrapper Discount

### V64 — Opposing-Auction Absorption Deficit

**protocol; Pre-return screen.** 335 balanced stable signals and full independent action agreement; only 73 in 2021 versus required 90. No P&L.

**What could change the evidence:** Measured but stopped by a mandate gate. Not a missing-data priority and not evidence of an economic failure.

**Sources:** [LEVERAGED_FUND_REBALANCE_ABSORPTION_AUDIT_V64.md](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/LEVERAGED_FUND_REBALANCE_ABSORPTION_AUDIT_V64.md), [LEVERAGED_FUND_REBALANCE_ABSORPTION_POSTMORTEM_V64.md](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/LEVERAGED_FUND_REBALANCE_ABSORPTION_POSTMORTEM_V64.md), [leveraged_fund_rebalance_absorption_returns_blind_audit_v64.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/leveraged_fund_rebalance_absorption_returns_blind_audit_v64.json)

### V65 — CFTC Participant Transfer times ES Opening

**robustness; Development; timing caveat.** Ending cash $101.47 versus SPY $132.40; cost, report-cluster and controls failed. Later V87 audit identified the 2023 CFTC publication outage requiring exclusions.

**What could change the evidence:** Historical actual report release dates, including outage, are needed for exact causal reconciliation. Old claim of universally safe lag is qualified; this does not promote the rule.

### V87 — CFTC Positions per Trader

**semantic; Pre-return screen.** 364 signals; 327 bullish/37 bearish; 95.6% agreement with plain net positions. No P&L; 2023 outage excluded.

**What could change the evidence:** Trader-count normalization did not identify a new mechanism; report availability is independently essential.

### ES:es_aggressor_causality_switch_prereg_v1 — ES Aggressor Causality Switch v1

**economic; Historical P&L.** Size-weighted next-transaction response did not distinguish flow worth following from flow worth fading. The frozen switch lost in every year. Its near-flat short result is not permission to select shorts or negate the switch.

**What could change the evidence:** Futures execution/capital is not interchangeable with a $100 cash-ETF account. Weak retained candidates require full holding-window MES quotes and fresh forward evidence; no outcome-driven repair.

**Original claim (not established causality):** Net aggressor flow has different meaning when aggressors are locally informed versus locally absorbed. The signed, size-weighted next-transaction price response estimates whether aggression is predictive. Follow net flow when that response is positive; fade net flow when it is negative.

**Relationship:** Shares ES history with other opening/value/flow variants; retained paper labels are research recommendations, not broker authorization.

**Sources:** [es_aggressor_causality_switch_prereg_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/es_aggressor_causality_switch_prereg_v1.json)

Recorded failed gates: development_gate_decision.positive_each_year: fail; development_gate_decision.both_directions_positive: fail; development_gate_decision.aggregate_profit_factor: fail.

### ES:es_aggressor_clock_compression_prereg_v1 — ES Aggressor Clock Compression v1

**economic; Historical P&L.** Relative aggressor-side burstiness was not directional. Fading the more clustered point process lost in every year and both directions. Following it, changing timestamps, or substituting another burstiness statistic would be result-derived rescue and is prohibited.

**What could change the evidence:** Futures execution/capital is not interchangeable with a $100 cash-ETF account. Weak retained candidates require full holding-window MES quotes and fresh forward evidence; no outcome-driven repair.

**Original claim (not established causality):** Aggressor sides are competing point processes. A side with more variable exchange-event interarrival times is arriving in compressed bursts rather than sustaining smooth participation. Near the pre-close boundary, that bursty side is more likely to represent deadline urgency that has already exhausted, so price should move against it into the close.

**Relationship:** Shares ES history with other opening/value/flow variants; retained paper labels are research recommendations, not broker authorization.

**Sources:** [es_aggressor_clock_compression_prereg_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/es_aggressor_clock_compression_prereg_v1.json)

Recorded failed gates: development_gate_decision.positive_each_year: fail; development_gate_decision.both_directions_positive: fail; development_gate_decision.aggregate_profit_factor: fail.

### ES:es_aggressor_granularity_inversion_prereg_v1 — ES Aggressor Granularity Inversion v1

**robustness; Historical P&L.** Fading the side with larger average aggressive trade size produced an interesting aggregate result, but essentially all profit came from shorts and 2022. The small 2021 and long-side losses still violate preregistered gates. A short-only or side-conditioned granularity engine is prohibited.

**What could change the evidence:** Futures execution/capital is not interchangeable with a $100 cash-ETF account. Weak retained candidates require full holding-window MES quotes and fresh forward evidence; no outcome-driven repair.

**Original claim (not established causality):** Aggressor volume loses information about granularity: many small trades and a few large trades can have identical volume. During the pre-close transfer, the side using the larger average aggressive trade reveals greater urgency and impact paid rather than superior information. Price should move against that larger-average-size aggressor side into the close.

**Relationship:** Shares ES history with other opening/value/flow variants; retained paper labels are research recommendations, not broker authorization.

**Sources:** [es_aggressor_granularity_inversion_prereg_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/es_aggressor_granularity_inversion_prereg_v1.json)

### ES:es_auction_arrow_of_time_prereg_v1 — ES Auction Arrow of Time v1

**economic; Historical P&L.** The time-antisymmetric placement of opening volume around adjacent price changes did not predict the later session. Both directions lost. Minute-volume lead-lag structure is either too noisy at one-minute resolution or does not persist beyond the opening auction. Negating or thresholding the score is prohibited.

**What could change the evidence:** Futures execution/capital is not interchangeable with a $100 cash-ETF account. Weak retained candidates require full holding-window MES quotes and fresh forward evidence; no outcome-driven repair.

**Original claim (not established causality):** Opening volume can be information-bearing when it arrives before displacement and reactive when it arrives after displacement. A time-antisymmetric volume-price statistic can distinguish the direction in which antecedent participation caused more subsequent movement than participation merely followed movement.

**Relationship:** Shares ES history with other opening/value/flow variants; retained paper labels are research recommendations, not broker authorization.

**Sources:** [es_auction_arrow_of_time_prereg_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/es_auction_arrow_of_time_prereg_v1.json)

### ES:es_auction_density_switch_prereg_v1 — ES Closing Auction-Density Switch v1

**economic; Historical P&L.** Volume per visited price level was not a stable acceptance measure. Both density states, every year, and both directions lost money.

**What could change the evidence:** Futures execution/capital is not interchangeable with a $100 cash-ETF account. Weak retained candidates require full holding-window MES quotes and fresh forward evidence; no outcome-driven repair.

**Original claim (not established causality):** Equal-duration closing-auction halves reveal whether a late price move was densely negotiated or crossed a liquidity vacuum. Rising volume per visited price level indicates acceptance and should preserve the second-half move; falling density indicates fragile displacement that should reverse.

**Relationship:** Shares ES history with other opening/value/flow variants; retained paper labels are research recommendations, not broker authorization.

### ES:es_auction_entropy_phase_transition_prereg_v1 — ES Auction Entropy Phase Transition v1

**robustness; Historical P&L.** The combined order/disorder state machine was profitable only because ordered transitions, shorts, and 2022 offset losing complements. Those subsets were revealed by the completed test and cannot be selected. The full preregistered entropy-transition hypothesis is temporally and directionally unstable.

**What could change the evidence:** Futures execution/capital is not interchangeable with a $100 cash-ETF account. Weak retained candidates require full holding-window MES quotes and fresh forward evidence; no outcome-driven repair.

**Original claim (not established causality):** The opening auction can transition between directional disorder and order. Falling binary direction entropy indicates spontaneous coordination and persistence of the second-half directional majority. Rising entropy indicates coordination is dissolving, so the second-half majority should be faded.

**Relationship:** Shares ES history with other opening/value/flow variants; retained paper labels are research recommendations, not broker authorization.

**Sources:** [es_auction_entropy_phase_transition_prereg_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/es_auction_entropy_phase_transition_prereg_v1.json)

### ES:es_auction_hysteresis_escape_prereg_v1 — ES Auction Hysteresis Escape v1

**economic; Historical P&L.** Price crossing the cash open while cumulative value lagged on the other side did not create an escape state; nearly every event reclaimed the cash-open stop. This exact away-from-value hypothesis is rejected. A fade toward value is an obvious inverse discovered only after the result and is explicitly prohibited from historical testing.

**What could change the evidence:** Futures execution/capital is not interchangeable with a $100 cash-ETF account. Weak retained candidates require full holding-window MES quotes and fresh forward evidence; no outcome-driven repair.

**Original claim (not established causality):** Cumulative volume-weighted value is auction memory, not necessarily a magnet. When price crosses the 09:30 cash open while the evolving session value center remains stranded on the opposite side, price and accepted value occupy incompatible states. Trading away from the stale value center should capture liquidation by inventory still benchmarked to the old state.

**Relationship:** Shares ES history with other opening/value/flow variants; retained paper labels are research recommendations, not broker authorization.

**Sources:** [es_auction_hysteresis_escape_prereg_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/es_auction_hysteresis_escape_prereg_v1.json)

### ES:es_auction_polarity_transfer_prereg_v1 — ES Auction Polarity Transfer v1

**economic; Historical P&L.** Reordering morning and lunch value centers across the cash open was not evidence that lunch controlled the close. The exact rule lost in every year and both directions.

**What could change the evidence:** Futures execution/capital is not interchangeable with a $100 cash-ETF account. Weak retained candidates require full holding-window MES quotes and fresh forward evidence; no outcome-driven repair.

**Original claim (not established causality):** When morning accepted value and lunch accepted value occupy opposite sides of the cash open, the anchor ordering records a true participant-group polarity reversal. If lunch's terminal price agrees with its center, the lunch group has taken control and the cash-open boundary should separate its inventory from the abandoned morning state.

**Relationship:** Shares ES history with other opening/value/flow variants; retained paper labels are research recommendations, not broker authorization.

### ES:es_closing_auction_value_ratchet_prereg_v1 — ES Closing Auction Value Ratchet v1

**robustness; Historical P&L.** Late-session price leading a migrated cumulative center was a 2022-dominated effect rather than a stable closing-handoff edge. The closing clock alone does not turn value migration into robust persistence. No year, side, distance, or volatility filter is permitted.

**What could change the evidence:** Futures execution/capital is not interchangeable with a $100 cash-ETF account. Weak retained candidates require full holding-window MES quotes and fresh forward evidence; no outcome-driven repair.

**Original claim (not established causality):** The opening auction establishes the cash session's initial accepted-value center. If the cumulative cash-session center has migrated away from it by 13:59 and price is already beyond the migrated center in the same direction, closing-session participants inherit directional inventory rather than a balanced book. That inventory should have positive convexity into the closing liquidity window.

**Relationship:** Shares ES history with other opening/value/flow variants; retained paper labels are research recommendations, not broker authorization.

### ES:es_impact_half_life_relay_prereg_v1 — ES Endogenous Impact Half-Life Relay v1

**robustness; Historical P&L.** The repair response was more informative than the original shock, but the complete state machine failed 2023 and longs. Selecting only repaired shocks or shorts would be post-hoc rescue.

**What could change the evidence:** Futures execution/capital is not interchangeable with a $100 cash-ETF account. Weak retained candidates require full holding-window MES quotes and fresh forward evidence; no outcome-driven repair.

**Original claim (not established causality):** A large endogenous price displacement is useful only through the market's subsequent repair response. If at least half of the selected displacement remains at the participant handoff, it represents accepted information and should persist. If less than half remains, the impact was temporary liquidity debt and should continue repairing.

**Relationship:** Shares ES history with other opening/value/flow variants; retained paper labels are research recommendations, not broker authorization.

**Sources:** [es_impact_half_life_relay_prereg_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/es_impact_half_life_relay_prereg_v1.json)

**economic; Historical P&L.** Incomplete correction is not scale-invariant. Repeating the midday inventory story in 30-minute auctions again isolated a 2022 opening-window effect. No side, year, or slot filter is permitted after observing these results.

### ES:es_midday_inventory_reload_v1 — ES Midday Inventory Reload v1

**robustness; Historical P&L.** The pristine holdout is positive in aggregate and the engine has low daily correlation with the opening portfolio, but 2024 and longs fail while uncertainty remains high. Preserve only as a one-MES supplemental paper engine with no side or year filters. It is not a validated standalone strategy.

**What could change the evidence:** Futures execution/capital is not interchangeable with a $100 cash-ETF account. Weak retained candidates require full holding-window MES quotes and fresh forward evidence; no outcome-driven repair.

**Original claim (not established causality):** A two-hour lunch move that opposes but does not erase the preceding two-hour morning move represents an incomplete inventory correction. The original morning side should tend to reload into the closing session.

**Relationship:** Shares ES history with other opening/value/flow variants; retained paper labels are research recommendations, not broker authorization.

### IEX-OF — IEX Order-Flow Predictiveness

**economic; Recorded scout / validation.** Mean signed midpoint -11.42 bps; crossing plus friction -41.94 bps. The signal failed before and after execution.

**What could change the evidence:** Consolidated tape is a measurement improvement, not permission to flip or refit this failed composite.

**Sources:** [report.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/data/parquet/alpaca_iex_orderflow_v1/development/report.json), [alpaca_iex_orderflow_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/alpaca_iex_orderflow_v1.json)

Recorded failed gates: gates.minimum_mean_signed_midpoint_bps: False; gates.bootstrap_lower_above_zero: False; gates.positive_each_symbol: False; gates.positive_after_top_5pct_sessions_removed: False; gates.positive_iex_crossing_after_extra_friction: False.

### BTC-PROB — BTC Five-Minute Probability Model

**pending; Recorded scout / validation.** A statistical directional model, not an executable trading strategy. Small probability discrimination does not establish an edge over market prices. Final-holdout accuracy 51.19%; Brier improvement only 0.000196 over constant prior.

**What could change the evidence:** Fresh executable market probabilities/quotes, calibrated net expected value and independent days.

**Sources:** [report.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/data/parquet/bitcoin_5m_probability_binance_v1/report.json), [bitcoin_5m_probability_binance_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/bitcoin_5m_probability_binance_v1.json)

### BTC-SQUEEZE — Confirmed Short-Squeeze Continuation

**protocol; Recorded scout / validation.** Returns-blind feasibility stopped the frozen intersection of funding, OI, premium and spot-flow states. No profitability test. All inputs passed implemented availability checks; only three stage-two setups across the complete sample versus 50 required trades. Primarily recurrence, not missing input.

**What could change the evidence:** Sparse conditions/availability must be distinguished; do not relax thresholds after seeing counts.

**Sources:** [report.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/data/parquet/bitcoin_confirmed_short_squeeze_continuation_v1/feasibility/report.json), [bitcoin_confirmed_short_squeeze_continuation_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/bitcoin_confirmed_short_squeeze_continuation_v1.json)

### BTC-UNWIND — Crowded Long Unwind

**robustness; Recorded scout / validation.** Only 15 selected days, none in 2022; uncertainty included losses. Positive pooled gross expectancy did not satisfy sample/robustness gates.

**What could change the evidence:** More independent occurrences, not a threshold optimized to create them.

**Sources:** [report.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/data/parquet/bitcoin_crowded_long_unwind_binance_v1/development/report.json), [bitcoin_crowded_long_unwind_binance_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/bitcoin_crowded_long_unwind_binance_v1.json)

Recorded failed gates: gates.minimum_selected_days: False; gates.bootstrap_lower_above_zero: False.

### BTC-CARRY — Delta-Neutral Funding Carry

**protocol; Recorded scout / validation.** 41 development trades passed, but 2023 validation produced zero qualifying trades. Seven-day holds are outside day trading.

**What could change the evidence:** Regime participation and funding/capital/quote execution; do not call development success a day-trading solution.

**Sources:** [report.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/data/parquet/bitcoin_delta_neutral_funding_carry_binance_v1/internal_validation/report.json), [report.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/data/parquet/bitcoin_delta_neutral_funding_carry_binance_v1/development/report.json), [bitcoin_delta_neutral_funding_carry_binance_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/bitcoin_delta_neutral_funding_carry_binance_v1.json)

### BTC-BASIS — Spot/Perpetual Basis Convergence

**economic; Recorded scout / validation.** Gross +1.03 bps of capital per selected event became -23.97 bps under conservative costs despite 68.8% contraction accuracy.

**What could change the evidence:** Small convergence is not enough for four-leg friction; no hypothetical midpoint fill rescue.

**Sources:** [report.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/data/parquet/bitcoin_spot_perp_basis_convergence_binance_v1/development/report.json), [bitcoin_spot_perp_basis_convergence_binance_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/bitcoin_spot_perp_basis_convergence_binance_v1.json)

Recorded failed gates: gates.positive_after_conservative_costs: False.

### BTC-CLOSE — BTC US-Session Close Momentum

**economic; Recorded scout / validation.** 621 selected sessions; mean gross -0.62 bps, conservative -30.62 bps. Price momentum did not transfer economically.

**What could change the evidence:** No identified new-data fix for this exact rule.

**Sources:** [report.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/data/parquet/bitcoin_us_session_close_momentum_binance_v1/development/report.json), [bitcoin_us_session_close_momentum_binance_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/bitcoin_us_session_close_momentum_binance_v1.json)

Recorded failed gates: gates.positive_mean_gross: False; gates.positive_each_chronological_half_gross: False; gates.bootstrap_lower_above_zero: False; gates.positive_after_conservative_costs: False.

### SEC-CONT — SEC Item 2.02 Stock Continuation

**economic; Recorded scout / validation.** 411 events; stock mean +5.24 bps gross and -4.76 bps after fixed costs; residual net -0.90 bps. Coverage and uncertainty gates also failed.

**What could change the evidence:** Original announcement content and prior expectations would be new information; filing presence and initial return alone were tested.

**Sources:** [report.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/data/parquet/sec_item_202_premarket_continuation_v1/development/report.json), [sec_item_202_premarket_continuation_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/sec_item_202_premarket_continuation_v1.json)

### FUND-REV — Fundamental Event Smart Reversal

**economic; Recorded scout / validation.** Gross -5.56%, wrong-sign near-zero rank IC, default net -43.47%. SEC-veto proxy was incomplete, but core signal already lacked gross edge.

**What could change the evidence:** News/expectation data could define a different signal; not a demonstrated repair for reversal.

**Sources:** [fundamental_event_smart_reversal_scout.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/docs/reports/fundamental_event_smart_reversal_scout.json)

### CARRY-TREND — Cross-Asset Carry plus Trend

**outside_daytrade_positive; Recorded scout / validation.** Public-data scout passed, net Sharpe 0.608 with SPY diversification utility. Daily futures carry/trend is outside the intraday mandate and not formally validated.

**What could change the evidence:** Raw-contract replication, roll/cost/capital checks; useful separate program, not day-trade proof.

**Sources:** [cross_asset_carry_trend_scout.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/docs/reports/cross_asset_carry_trend_scout.json)

### IBB — IBB Afternoon Recovery

**economic; Recorded scout or proposal.** 124 trades; mean gross -0.092%, net -0.192%; both chronological halves negative. An individual ETF was already tested.

**What could change the evidence:** No post-loss threshold, time or ETF substitution.

**Sources:** [IBBIntradayAfternoonRecovery-v1-results.md](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/docs/research_scout/IBBIntradayAfternoonRecovery-v1-results.md)

### SPY-CORR — SPY Opening-Hour Correlation

**economic; Recorded scout or proposal.** 721 sessions; first/last Pearson 0.0285, Spearman -0.0391; conditional spread negative. This is a descriptive prediction failure, not a net-P&L backtest.

**What could change the evidence:** No demonstrated payoff edge; more attractive clock selection is not independent evidence.

**Sources:** [SPYOpeningHourCorrelation-v1-results.md](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/docs/research_scout/SPYOpeningHourCorrelation-v1-results.md)

### POLY-PROXY — BTC Model to Polymarket Outcome Transfer

**pending; Recorded scout or proposal.** 3,951 matched outcomes over 14 already-exposed days; slight Brier improvement with interval including zero. No contemporaneous executable decision prices.

**What could change the evidence:** Forward paired model probabilities and executable asks/depth; label accuracy alone cannot measure trading edge.

**Sources:** [Bitcoin5mPolymarketProxyCheck-v1.md](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/docs/research_scout/Bitcoin5mPolymarketProxyCheck-v1.md)

### BTC-CAPITULATION — Binance Capitulation to Coinbase Spot

**pending; Recorded scout or proposal.** Frozen proposal located; no completed result artifact found for this exact cross-venue rule.

**What could change the evidence:** Establish source/target price alignment and availability before evaluating returns.

**Sources:** [bitcoin_binance_capitulation_coinbase_spot_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/bitcoin_binance_capitulation_coinbase_spot_v1.json)

### IREN — IREN Two-Month Reversal

**scope; Recorded scout or proposal.** Two-month return-recurrence study lies outside the day-trading objective; retained for complete repository history. All four primary success gates failed: raw 42-day lag correlation 0.00317, negative-correlation permutation p=0.599, adjusted p=0.288. This was a descriptive recurrence test, not a completed trading P&L.

**What could change the evidence:** Do not present a multi-month result as intraday evidence.

**Original claim (not established causality):** IREN has a stable roughly two-month boom-bust tendency: a 42-trading-day return predicts an opposite-signed next 42-trading-day return, including after adjustment for Bitcoin and QQQ returns.

**Sources:** [results.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/experiments/iren_two_month_reversal_v1/results.json), [iren_two_month_reversal_prereg_v1.json](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/iren_two_month_reversal_prereg_v1.json)

Recorded failed gates: success_gate_checks.raw_negative_p_lt_0_05: False; success_gate_checks.adjusted_negative_p_lt_0_05: False; success_gate_checks.opposite_sign_rate_gte_0_60: False; success_gate_checks.both_halves_negative: False; success_gate_passed: False.

### V15-R1 — Early Closing-Rebalance Footprint

**semantic; Proposal or causal screen.** Unsigned ETF share volume did not measure derivative hedge demand; weaker proxy for already-tested closing flow.

**What could change the evidence:** Later AUM/flow acquisitions led to V61–V67; those outcomes supersede the old claim that all these inputs remain absent.

**Relationship:** V63; V67

**Sources:** [UPRO_SPXU_DESK_GRADE_HYPOTHESIS_SCREEN_V15.md](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/UPRO_SPXU_DESK_GRADE_HYPOTHESIS_SCREEN_V15.md)

### V15-R3 — Primary/Secondary-Market Convexity Gap

**data; Proposal or causal screen.** Intraday NAV/baskets and financing marks unavailable; trade-price residual not proof of AP imbalance.

**What could change the evidence:** Daily NAV/shares were later acquired. Intraday basket valuation and actual hedge demand remain different missing objects.

**Relationship:** V59–V62; V68–V70

**Sources:** [UPRO_SPXU_DESK_GRADE_HYPOTHESIS_SCREEN_V15.md](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/UPRO_SPXU_DESK_GRADE_HYPOTHESIS_SCREEN_V15.md)

### V15-R4 — Futures-Cash Basis Relay

**data; Proposal or causal screen.** Asynchronous minute prints cannot measure executable basis; a one-legged index bet mismatches the hedge.

**What could change the evidence:** Synchronized quotes, fair-carry inputs and realistic two-leg execution; fast arbitrage economics remain unproven.

**Relationship:** V90 is maturity spread, not the same basis

**Sources:** [UPRO_SPXU_DESK_GRADE_HYPOTHESIS_SCREEN_V15.md](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/UPRO_SPXU_DESK_GRADE_HYPOTHESIS_SCREEN_V15.md)

### V15-R5 — Constituent-Breadth Internalization Gap

**data; Proposal or causal screen.** Historical membership, corporate actions and timestamped opening information absent.

**What could change the evidence:** Later first-hour panels improve breadth but not point-in-time membership; V75–V77 failed different specific constructions.

**Relationship:** V75–V77

**Sources:** [UPRO_SPXU_DESK_GRADE_HYPOTHESIS_SCREEN_V15.md](C:/Hazem/quant_project/untitled_project/research_scout/UPRO_SPXU_DESK_GRADE_HYPOTHESIS_SCREEN_V15.md)

### V35-R1 — Final Auction-Sign Reversal

### DIAG-TSM-REPLAY — ETF TSM Runtime Replay

**diagnostic; Simulation replay.** Operational replay passed structural checks, but research ending equity $50,944 and replay mark-to-market $27,759 differ materially. It is not a second alpha validation or proof of economic replication.

**What could change the evidence:** Reconcile sizing, target timing, costs and constraints before presenting research returns as deployable runtime returns.

**Relationship:** Validated daily ETF TSM family
